# product decisions
- 2026-10-15 (Head of Product, Collection spec §9): P1 sim flags eggs_per_tap > 50% coop cap (none flagged); P2 "Limited by coop" hint; Q1 production at full coop is lost; Q2 tap_combine_window_ms=250, max_flying_eggs=5 as Collection tunables in economy.md; Q3 coop float, floor for display/taps (Eng confirmed); Q4 vibration out of M1 (backlog); Q5 <60 s background catch-up at online rate capped by coop, ≥60 s offline path, negative delta = 0, real-elapsed tick (Eng confirmed).
- 2026-10-15 (Head of Product, Workers m2-proposal v0.2): AC-W2 approved at ≤ +1 min vs M1 at F10/F18 for seller15 managed. Reason: the remaining +0.8/+1.0 min is hire cost, not fees, and it buys F25 90.8 → 62.8 min. AC-W4 approved at steps ≤ 6 min F18→F25. Reason: ≤ 3 min is unreachable with any tier set modelled (worst 6.0 with M2_REC, 4–5 even at ×6). Added soft, non-gating AC-W4b: cheapest-first steps ≤ 8 min (AC7-soft pattern; currently 7.8). Confirmed: workers do nothing offline in M2, offline stays 25% / 2 h (CEO rule: active play out-earns idle).
- 2026-10-15 (Head of Product, Workers m2-proposal v1.0): v1.0 approved with one change: the Hawker +1 queue slot is removed entirely, along with its "sold out" display. Reason: it moved pacing by ≤ 0.4 min, and a purely cosmetic slot would confuse players. Rule: the Workers handoff to Engineering happens only after QA signs off on M1.
- 2026-10-15 (Head of Product, Workers m2-proposal v1.1): v1.1 (§10) approved as written: 15 min drain (−1%/9 s), Tired < 30%, 40% yawn cue, Manage all at 3+ workers with a shared 30 s cooldown, asleep Grader keeps 15% (was 25%), AC-W5 = ≥ 1 Manage action per 15 min while any worker is hired, playtest metric managed/unmanaged F21 income ≥ 1.5 for active cohorts. The §8 QA rules clarifications stand. Note: the v1.0 handoff to Engineering was sanctioned by Head of Product and the CEO after QA signed off on M1 (m1-rc2).
- 2026-10-16 (Head of Product, Workers P4): approved. Manage is enabled only when a worker is at ≤ 40% energy (yawn point); the refill stays free; 30 s shared cooldown unchanged. Manage all counts only workers at ≤ 40% (needs 3+, refills only those), which fixes the bug where it counted workers at 81%+. Replaces the earlier Manage rule. Re-sim (product/playtest1-resim.md): AC-W4 5.5, AC-W4b 7.5, AC-W5 5.62 Manage taps / 15 min, all PASS.
- 2026-10-16 (PM Workers, rc1 S2-2a/S2-2b): "Manage all" renamed "Refill tired (N)" (N = workers at ≤ 40%; aria-label "Refill N tired workers"). Hidden when N = 0 and no cooldown is running; greyed with cooldown ring and "Ready in Ns" caption (≤ 1 update/s) during the 30 s cooldown. Per-worker Manage shows a one-line caption when greyed: "Not tired yet" (> 40%) or "Ready in Ns" (cooldown). Captions 12px Nunito #5A4C42 (Art & UX). P4 rules unchanged.
- 2026-10-16: M4 abilities spec v0.1 (product/workers/m4-abilities-spec.md). Confirmed HoP's paused and tired rules. Added: asleep or unpaid workers are blocked, and cooldowns tick while paused or offline. Magnitudes are pending Economy/Balance modeling.
- 2026-10-16: Pause recovery locked at 0.25× drain (~1%/36 s), confirmed by Economy (m4-tracks-model.md, 'Pause recovery sweep'). 0.35 is the upper bound. The seller15 break-even is accepted.
- 2026-10-16: Abilities v0.2. W1-W3 now raise price (W1, W3) or supply (W2) because the earlier versions added about 0% with supply as the limit. Pending Economy's sweep.
- 2026-10-16: Abilities v0.3 sizes set to x1.15/1.15/1.15 plus +0.5 tier, provisional on Economy's worst-seed check against the 2x active/idle cap.
- 2026-10-16: Abilities v0.3 sizes confirmed under the 2x cap (worst seed 1.95). Sent to Head of Product for approval.
- 2026-10-16: rc1 asleep-state spec written (workers/rc1-asleep-state-spec.md) in response to Game Critic #3.
- 2026-10-16: The rc1 HUD, cards and ETA use the incomePerSec selector (Farm Upgrades clarity spec v0.3). Alignment closed.

- 2026-10-16 13:55 rc1 UI: Cut A approved (shared incomePerSec + full asleep + Farm/Collection chips + bestBuy goal + Collection ETA/link; worker/Grader chips, UC11, upgrade_bought, >=960 chips -> rc2). Fallback Cut B. ETA copy "Affordable in ~m:ss". bestBuy assumes Working; asleep/unpaid best-buy worker shows "Refill". Workers badge on bottom nav.

- 2026-10-16 13:56 Market critic triage approved: rc1 T1/T2/T4 + MK-34..39; T5->Workers; T6 folded into shared incomePerSec (a11y text same source); T3/T7/T8 rc2/M4.

- 2026-10-16 13:57 M4 columns: economy.f1-m4cols.csv lands in both CSVs at the F1 merge; Eng reads it directly until then. collection_price_bonus flips to 0 in the M4 change. Ability cols added.

- 2026-10-16 14:10 M4-A gate passed 9/9; M4-A KICKED OFF (CEO pre-approved). QA Gamer leads: Collection label copy -> PM Collection; disabled Refill reason AC -> PM Workers; L14 dead zone verify on F1 build (QA+Economy).
- 2026-10-16: rc1 adds AC-R8 (caption on the disabled Refill button) and AC-R9 (T5 plural fix).

## 2026-10-16 14:45 — M4-A review verdicts (Head of Product)
Source: product/m4a-review-response.md (Economy, checked against its own models) and the Claude Design Reviewer's report.
- B1 APPROVED: at migration only, V may go over the F+3 cap. Search V 0-30 with no min(F+3,30). Further V buys stay blocked until F+3>=V. No new save field. Update the M4A-28 text. Fixtures: F0/C5->V4, F3/C10->V7, F5/C14->V9, F13/C30->V17.
- M1 APPROVED: Hens milestones stop raising lay rate and raise coop size and offline eggs only (the hens_ms columns get a new meaning). Accepted that idle income at a given level drops about 20-30%, since active over idle stays 1.53-1.61.
- M2 ACCEPTED: the 1.61x peak on C>=F+4 saves counts as part of the 10-minute catch-up burst we already accepted.
- M3: extend TR-5 to 60 min. Economy proposes about -25% on F22-26 Farm and Egg value costs, and that needs my approval before any CSV change.
- M4: the spec text is wrong. Correct it to x7.8 (1.5 x 1.3 x 4.0, including Balanced).
- 1 cent: compare in integer cents with one half-up rounding step in migration.

## 2026-10-16 14:46 — M4-A follow-ups (Head of Product)
- M4A-27 APPROVED: on load, clamp only above level 30. Caps are enforced at purchase. No new save field.
- TR-5 at 60 min added to M4A-35 now as a ship gate for M4-A. It is a known failure at F25 (a 4.0 min gap) until Economy's F22-26 cost proposal is approved.
- The M4A-12 and M4A-22 milestone messages need new wording from Art & UX (coop size and offline eggs, not a laying boost).

## 2026-10-16 14:52 — M3 approved, with a condition (Head of Product)
- farm_cost and egg_value_cost at L24-29 x0.70 (Economy's proposal: 60-min worst gap 4.00 -> 2.75 min, 30/30 seeds).
- Condition: costs must keep rising level to level. L24 can't cost less than L23 (x0.70 on L24 likely does). If it does, taper the cut from L22 (for example x0.9/0.8/0.7) and re-run the 60-min check, keeping every gap at 3.0 min or less.
- Re-check once workers and the Grader are modelled.
- M2 is closed: worst first-minute boost is 1.35x under the Hens fix.

## 2026-10-16 14:54 — M4-A r2 majors (Head of Product)
- MJ1: the first offline grant after migration uses a Hens offline multiplier of x1.0, because that time away was under the old rules. Spec line 174 must say this.
- MJ2: offline eggs = idle eggs/s (the shared incomePerSec rate with no taps) x min(away, 2 h) x hens_offline_mult. The 2 h time cap is fixed; offline_max_eggs_2h scales with hens_offline_mult. Name the column in M4A-4.
- Economy re-checks offline income at 2 h against the active-over-idle range of 1.53-1.61 after MJ2, and flags it if the ratio drops below 1.4.

## 2026-10-16 14:56 — M3 final (Head of Product)
- APPROVED as in the CSV: Farm and Egg value costs L23 x0.90, L24-29 x0.70. Costs rise strictly from level to level. 60-min worst gap 2.75 min (30/30 seeds); the 30-min and casual runs pass.
- The Grader top tier stays at 51,567 and isn't re-linked. It's a separate purchase, and it gives players something to buy in that zone.
- f1-m4cols is the source of truth for L23-29. economy.f1.csv must be marked superseded at those rows so the two don't drift apart.

## 2026-10-16 14:57 — Active vs offline target (Head of Product)
- No active-vs-offline target in M4-A; it belongs with the rest-of-M4 offline work. The only M4-A guardrail is that active over idle (tab open, no taps) stays at 1.4 or more.

## 2026-10-16 14:58 — Pacing band with workers (Head of Product)
- Separate band for runs with workers: Farm level at 30:00 between L20 and L24. The no-worker band stays L18-22. Workers are meant to speed play up, and the worker model is optimistic, so the upper bound is there to catch runaway pacing.
- The M3 cut and the Grader at 51,567 are final after the workers + Grader re-check (every gap 3.0 min or less, 30/30 seeds).

## 2026-10-16 15:14 — Sell button format, MK-24-1 vs BUG-1/2 (Head of Product)
- APPROVED, Engineering's proposal: the button shows the short format (1.2K/3.4M) built from the same payout cents. The short format rounds down, so it never shows more than gets credited. Below $1K it shows exact cents. The aria label, the '+$X' popup and the 'Thanks! +$X' card stay exact to the cent. The AC is now: same payout function, and the displayed value is the exact cents rounded down to the display precision.
- rc2 backlog: rename the 'Collect per tap' card to match its main benefit, egg price (PM Collection's idea; Art & UX to write the copy).

## 2026-10-16 15:15 — Save durability rules (Head of Product)
- If the main save is missing and a valid .bak exists: restore from .bak. Never start a new farm over it, and never overwrite a valid .bak with a fresh state.
- A null savedAt counts as missing: 0 offline earnings, stamped with now, farm kept (QA-SD-5).
- A savedAt of 0 or below, or one in the future, is invalid: 0 offline earnings, stamped with now, farm kept.

- Highlight and the next-goal hint use one rule: best payback (rc1 bestBuy). The highlight goes on the best-payback affordable row. If nothing is affordable, the hint shows the best-payback row with its ETA.
- collection_price_bonus 0.12xC confirmed as intended: the code ignores it when the flag is on, and migration reads it for the v6 price.

## 2026-10-16 16:08 — Highlight and hint switching (Head of Product)
- One rule for the M4A-18 highlight and the UC6 hint: switch only when the new pick's payback is at least 10% better, and hold at least 2 s.
- Switch at once (no threshold) when the current pick is bought or stops being affordable.
- Re-evaluate on state change, with UC6's 5 s re-check as the fallback. PM Farm updates the spec; Engineering builds it.

## 2026-10-16 16:15 — Picker scope (Head of Product, approving PM Farm's ruling)
- The hint ranks over every option; the Farm-tab highlight ranks over Farm-tab rows only. Both use the same picker (at least 10% better payback, 2 s hold), and they must match whenever the overall best is a Farm-tab row. This keeps UC7 (Collection hint on a fresh save).
- A rebuy of the same row keeps its 2 s count.
- A row that becomes affordable from nothing skips the 2 s hold but must still clear the 10% bar.

## 2026-10-16 19:40 — B-1 full-coop stall, and goal-label truncation (Head of Product)
- B-1 (Major): the top-bar rate shows real net income per second (realized sales minus wages), never theoretical production. If the coop is full, both the rate and the coop show a "Coop full" state. The goal ETA uses that real net rate; at 0 or below it shows "—" instead of a frozen value. A hint says "Coop full: collect eggs or hire a Coop Hand", using the same best-payback picker. Owner: PM Collection (spec + ACs, with PM Workers for wages), then Engineering's UI owner. rc2 Major.
- C-AC26b stands: the goal label is never truncated. If it doesn't fit, it wraps to 2 lines, then uses the defined short track name. The Art & UX v1.4 ellipsis rule doesn't apply to the goal label. Art & UX reconciles the copy spec.
- 19:40 amendment (CEO direction): B-1 is a must-fix for rc1, not rc2. Spec owner is PM Workers, with PM Collection consulting on the coop state, then Engineering builds it. The ruling itself is unchanged.

## 2026-10-16 21:56 — M4A-31(f)1 when IndexedDB is unavailable (Head of Product)
- APPROVED (matches ADR M4-16): if IndexedDB is unavailable (private mode, open fails, or open takes >2000 ms), the migration goes ahead with the required localStorage v6_backup alone and fires save_backup_idb_unavailable {reason, path}.
- If IndexedDB is available but the write fails, the migration aborts and play continues on v6.
- PM Farm adds the spec note and ACs.

## 2026-10-16 22:05 — m4a-spec v1.6 applied (PM Farm)
- Spec aligned with engineering/m4-design.md v0.2.8 ADR M4-8: SAVE_VERSION stays 6 for M4-A (in-place `tracksV7: 1` marker after a one-time `eggfarm.save.v6_backup`); v7 bump is S-4. Per HoP 21:56 / ADR M4-16, M4A-31(f)1 split (IDB unavailable → LS-only + `save_backup_idb_unavailable`; IDB write fails → abort, stay v6, retry next load) with ACs (g)/(h). No new product decision; record of the edit.
- 21:59 amendment (replaces the 2000 ms rule): migrate on localStorage alone ONLY when IndexedDB is definitely unavailable (no indexedDB global, or the open throws or rejects with a private-mode or security error). If the open takes >=1500 ms, fails any other way, or a read fails, the migration is DEFERRED: the save stays v6, save_migration_aborted {reason: idb_timeout | idb_read_error} fires, and the next load retries. A write failure or readback_mismatch aborts, as in (h). Added: save_migration_aborted carries attempt_count, and 3 or more deferrals in a row on one device gets flagged in telemetry for review (no forced migration). Owner: PM Farm.

## 2026-10-16 22:20 — m4a-spec v1.7 applied (PM Farm)
- HoP 21:59 amendment written into M4A-31(f)1/(g)/(d1)/(h) and M4A-34. PM Farm choice: the stuck flag is a field `stuck_on_v6: true` on `save_migration_aborted` when attempt_count ≥ 3 (no separate event). attempt_count LS key proposed as `eggfarm.save.migration_attempts` (Engineering to confirm). Hung IDB write after a successful open uses the same T = 1500 ms (was 2000 ms).

## 2026-10-16 22:04 — M4 migration spec notes, from Code Reviewer N8 (Head of Product)
APPROVED; PM Farm writes them into the spec before S-3.
- M4A-30: the migration commits in-session, so the boot offline grant runs under v6 rules at x1.0, consistent with MJ1. S-5 expects 19,800 at boot and 24,750 (x1.25) on the next load.
- M4A-33: the explainer shows when the migration commits in-session, not on the first M4 load.
- The backup record is {migrationId, raw}; an orphan backup is overwritten only by the next migration.
- 22:09 amendment (Head of Product): APPROVED. onblocked defers with reason idb_blocked and attempt_count++. 'Unavailable' = no_global or security (SecurityError, InvalidStateError, private-mode errors). During a deferral session only localStorage saves. Spec v1.9 (PM Farm).
- 22:13 amendment (Head of Product): v1.10 APPROVED. IDB backup writes and the {migrationId, raw} record move to S-4; M4-A makes zero IDB writes. quota_headroom is a deferral (attempt_count++). Threshold: defer unless localStorage can fit the v7 save plus the v6_backup with at least 2x margin (that is, free space >= 2 x (new save + backup) bytes), checked by a trial write. The Architect owns how it's measured.

Restore the newest one that validates and show a toast "We restored your farm from a backup." **D-HOP-28 (2026-10-19 09:44):** M4B-1 lock label rule (pending UI QA confirmation). A track row locked by 'cap' shows "Needs Farm L{N}". N is the smallest F' > F where trackCap(F', track) > the current track level, using the existing hensCap/eggValueCap in tracks.ts. If no farm level qualifies, it falls back to farmMax and a test asserts that this can't happen. The aria-label uses the same N. For normal saves N = F+1, so nothing changes for them. The fix is UI-only, adds no economy change, and is in M4.1 scope as a bug fix. Telemetry note: PR #53 (M41-10) changes the build id prefix to m4.1. No Product dashboard or sim parses m3-rc1-pre (grep showed only docs and scratch fixtures), so Product has nothing to do.

**D-HOP-29 (2026-10-19 09:52):** F4 next-goal wording for M41-8. There's no real conflict, because the two strings belong to different places. The next-goal strip, for a Hens or Egg value target, shows `★ {Track} L{n} · +X% income`, per m3-next-goal-spec §1 and rc1-upgrade-value.md §3–4. Truncation keeps the ≤27-character cap and applies in order: drop the ★, then use the short track name from rc1 §4, then put an ellipsis on the track name. The `+X% income` part is never cut, and the level number is never cut. The m4-tracks-copy.md strings (`Hens L{n}: bigger coop · offline ×{m}` and similar) are for milestone toasts and the in-row "Next at L{n}" hint only, never the strip. Screen readers read the untruncated label. ACs: (1) `Egg value L30 · +12% income` (27) drops only the ★; (2) a 28+ character label switches to the short name; (3) no rendered label is longer than 27; (4) toasts and hints are unchanged. Art & UX can object by 10:30, otherwise this is final. P41-5 (folding F3/F4 into m4a-spec) can come after the build but must be done by 17:15 so QA tests against one spec.
- 09:55 amendment to D-HOP-29: Art & UX's version in art-ux/f4-next-goal-label.md is adopted. When the level being bought is a milestone, the strip shows `★ {Track} L{n}: {milestone}`, and the milestone replaces the %. Otherwise it shows `★ {Track} L{n} · +{X}% income`. X = floor(100 × (income/s after this one buy − income/s now) / income/s now) at the current state. It uses the same income model and the same value best_roi uses for the pick, with no separate calculation. If X < 1, the % part is hidden. If income/s now = 0, the % is hidden too. Truncation is unchanged, and the milestone text is never cut. Added AC: for a fixed state, the strip's X equals floor(best_roi's computed income delta %) in a unit test.
- 10:00 final for D-HOP-29 (messages crossed): the milestone variant from the 09:55 amendment stands. Art & UX's edit is adopted. If 0 < delta < 1%, the strip shows `+<1% income`, never `+0%`. The % is hidden only when the income delta is exactly 0 or income/s now is 0. Art & UX restores the milestone branch in art-ux/f4-next-goal-label.md. That file is the source of truth.

**D-HOP-30 (2026-10-19 10:00):** CSR-17 field-level save repair is approved with changes, as a stretch item inside M4.1 scope item 3 (CSR). It applies only when the save parses and its version and required structure are valid, and only to an enumerated list of level fields that fail sanitizeLevel. Repair value: the lowest value the rest of the save implies (the max of the implied lower bounds), or 0 if nothing implies one. We never repair upward to a higher guess, so a corruption can't become an exploit. Coins and every other valid field are kept, and the original bytes go to corrupt.*. Any other failing field, or more than 3 bad level fields, falls through to the existing full fallback chain. The player sees a non-blocking notice listing what was reset, with the existing export option. Telemetry: save_repaired {fields[], count, version}. Gate: Saves engineering confirms it's buildable and HoQA confirms it's testable by 12:00. Otherwise CSR-17 moves to M4.2, and CSR v0.2.3 ships without it. It must not put the 17:15 handoff at risk.

**D-HOP-32 (2026-10-19 10:05):** These are rulings on PM Farm Upgrades' open questions in m4a-spec v1.18 §20.

2) OQ-F3-1 is a bug and is fixed in M4.1. Emit tracks_explainer_shown and tracks_explainer_dismissed {method: button|tap|esc|timeout} per M4A-34. It goes in with M41-9.
3) OQ-F4-1: the short name stays 'Value'. The f4 file overrides rc1 §4 for track names. OQ-F4-6: the % and milestone text must never be cut, including by CSS. Only the track name sits in a span that can shrink and get an ellipsis, and the suffix span can't shrink. AC: at a 360px viewport, the full suffix is visible for the worst-case string.
4) OQ-F4-2: Collection keeps its existing template `Collection L{n}: +{Δtap}/tap`, with ★ when it's the best buy and the same truncation order. The +X% income template is for Hens and Egg value only, because tap gain isn't passive income/s. Art & UX should change the f4 file's worst-case example to a track string.
CSR-17 stays conditional per D-HOP-30. It is cut to M4.2 if M41-7 isn't green by about 15:00.

Those seeds are then gated from their divergence point. L0–30 CSV byte-exactness (G7) is unchanged. Economy runs (a) only as information. Neither (a) nor (b) ships without a ruling.

## D-HOP-2026-10-24-04 · 2026-10-24 16:10 CEST · HoP (M5.0 spec-gap rulings, per HoE engineering/m5.0-sizing.md §8)
1. Credited tap (one definition for streak, A1 tap bonus, telemetry): a tap on the coop counts if ≥1.0 s of sim time has passed since the last credited tap, whether or not eggs move.
2. Reference income for A1/B1/Broody: trailing 60 s of sim-time passive income only (workers, Grader, milestones, egg-value grades). Excludes golden-egg cash, frenzy, abilities, Broody, tap bonus, so no self-feeding loop. Hidden-tab handling superseded by D-HOP-2026-10-24-07 X2.
3. Streak cap: 0.28 provisional (R9) until Economy G3; CSV 0.40 and CEO first-pass 0.30 are superseded by the G3 result.
4. Modals, sheets, explainer, onboarding and coach freeze the golden-egg timer and the streak decay together; nothing decays while a menu is open.
5. "Attended" = tab visible and a player input within the last 30 s (sim time). Used by abilities and any attended-only rule.
6. Golden egg: Art v1.0 spawn spots/mocks are final; asset file is golden-egg.svg. M4-AC24 restated against G1 (P95 ≤ 2.0, worst seed ≤ 2.25); focus and M4-B references removed.
7. Broody Hen unlocks at the first Coop Hand hire; depends on #69's yard landing in main.
8. Abilities: Rush applies to Market Hand sales only. Sweep F20+ effect (lay vs price) is decided by PM Workers + Economy in G3.
9. Pause/Resume: CEO D-CEO-20261024-06 overrides my R13 drop. In scope as the last PR, behind a flag, first to cut. A paused worker recovers energy at the 0.25× rate (locked, decisions.md:9; wording corrected 16:20 per HoQA); no Grader bonus while paused.
10. New CSV columns (HoE §8.2) are drafted by Economy with G3 and applied only after HoP approval; implementation hold stands.

## D-HOP-2026-10-24-07 · 2026-10-24 16:20 CEST · HoP (HoQA M5.0 testability, qa/m5/testability-gaps.md)
X1 Yes: QA checks each feature's income gain within ±20% of Economy's G3 prediction. X2 Keep RC1: hidden <60 s is simulated live, ≥60 s is offline. While hidden or a menu is open the golden-egg timer and streak decay freeze (no spawns, no decay). Hidden <60 s counts in the 60 s reference-income window; after an offline return, and until 60 s of sim time accrue, reference income = steady-state passive rate (also answers ST-2). After an offline return (≥60 s hidden) the streak resets to 0 (ST-3/4: under a menu or short hide the streak value is preserved exactly). X3 Approved: seeds 0–99, ±1 tick, ±1¢, ~3σ bands; golden-egg rate asserted against the no-catch baseline (~15/h) with catch variants as separate cases. X4 M5.0 includes only the telemetry M5.0 features and the active/idle gate need (incl. sid/fgMs on those events, ability events on the allowlist, ability_blocked rate-limited); the full v0.6.2 migration is a separate item HoE sizes and the CEO schedules. X6 Yes: "never stack" means only the parked M4-B rewarded boost. X7 Sale value = base price × egg-value grade × Grader × balanced bonus × streak × frenzy, multiplied unrounded and rounded once to the cent at sale; tap coins and Broody pay separately, each rounded once; Economy confirms the order in G3.

## D-COL-2026-10-24-01 · 2026-10-24 17:22 CEST · PM, Collection (Broody Hen minimum nest slots)
- **Decision:** the minimum acceptable eligible nest-slot count at any supported viewport is **1**. 959×600's 2 slots (Y0, Y5; Art rects 5d9efa6 §C) are acceptable.

### D-HOP-2026-10-24-34 · ~19:06 CEST (stamp corrected 19:11; was 19:14) · Pause × golden timer, variant (ii) rules (answers qa/m5/delta-pause-golden-timer.md Q1–Q7; draft until CEO change approval)
Q1: the freeze holds while ANY hired worker is paused (Pause flag on, F ≥ 10). It lifts on the tick the last paused worker resumes, including a Manage resume. Q2: only the golden spawn countdown, the on-screen egg age and frenzyRemainingS freeze; focusClock, streak, Broody and abilities are untouched. Q3: an egg on screen stays visible in the frozen/paused style and cannot be tapped while frozen. Q4: Frenzy ×2 does NOT apply while frozen; timer and effect are suspended together, otherwise pausing extends Frenzy. This supersedes Pause E14, golden wins. Q5: QA's ordering is accepted: pause applies before the golden age/countdown update, so there is no spawn or expiry on the pause tick, and a fade already in progress completes. Q6: QA's PGT-6/6b/6c/7/8 are accepted. Reload or offline while paused stays frozen, and the existing R8 reload-expire rule is unchanged. Q7: telemetry is minimal: session_end.golden_frozen_s plus worker_paused.golden_on_screen and frenzy_active, with HoE allowlist. Profiles: every §10 G5-P profile including slow, seeds 1–200, 8dc90e2b, lockout 45. Owners: PM Collection (golden) and PM Workers (Pause E14 rewrite) co-draft; Econ re-runs (ii) on these rules.

The worst ≤ 180 warning is still the pre-existing dead zone (D-HOP-30). BR-AC28 passes: post-L30 hatch gap P95 109–122 s, worst 132–152 s. Farm-first is the reference hatch policy for breeds; whenidle is a known trap (it fails G4). Ruling for every G4 definition from M5.1 on: "affordable" counts only rows with a positive suffix/gain. Zero-gain rows never close a gap. This is a BB-10 gate-definition item in the BBv2 proposal. Economy confirms whether the M5.0 X1 G4 rows (§13.2a) already exclude zero-gain rows; if they don't, it reports the re-measured values, and the plan stays frozen pending CEO review. Under BBv2 on meaningful rows, P95 is 197–199 (> 180) and the worst seed is +5 s. That is a known M5.1 risk for the L0–30 unfreeze.

The UI must state the wage that keeps running ("Asleep · still paid $X/min"), so it is never a hidden drain. There is no CSV change, and it stays on paper until the unfreeze. My position on PMW's Q-WF-1: accept "≤ everywhere, < wherever the worker has non-redundant work", because a redundant worker tying is honest, and inventing output for it isn't.
  - **Q-HoP-1:** From M5.1, the G1 idle comparator of record is the idle bot with autoRefill ON. A real idle player turns on the idle feature, so the gate measures that player. The result is that tc_autorefill_reserve = 0, and reserve 1 is legal only with the Asleep waiver. Reserve 0 is preferred.
  - **Q-HoP-2:** AC-SC8 as written ("≥20% of L10–17 charge-starved") is retired. It was measured on the pre-M4 curve, it already fails with the flag off, and L10–17 lasts only about 6 min. The replacement AC: with C2 on, the charge-starved share over L10–30 per session is ≥ the flag-off share on the same seeds, which means auto-refill must not erase scarcity. Econ proposes any absolute target from measured data at the unfreeze. There's no regen retune to chase a stale number.
  - **Q-HoP-3:** The G4 baseline for C2 is M5.1 flag-off on the same harness and seeds (1–200, 8dc90e2b, meaningful rows per D-HOP-35). The rc2 204 s figure is information only. Casual worst 230 s with auto vs 216 s base is a Y FAIL (+14 s) unless the harness of record re-run clears it. C2 must close it through tunables or a rule before approval, with no waiver.

### D-HOP-2026-10-25-01 — Workers M5.1 shipping re-run rulings (Econ Q-HoP-3, D1–D3) · 2026-10-25 01:30 CEST
Evidence: workers/reviews/m5.1-shipping-rerun-econ.md sha1 05f8e1939518667529772d6c68d403d5fd912783 (seeds 1–200, CSV 8dc90e2b, reserve 0, no auto-rest, clock wage on).
- **D1 (C2 fix) — REJECTED as stated.** Restating the band to "auto <= manual + 1%" removes the reason the band exists (manual play must out-earn auto-refill). at_pct 30 is accepted as the G4 setting, but the band (85–97%) must be restored with a second lever. Econ runs one bounded sweep at at_pct 30 over levers that do not touch the Farm-save window: (a) auto-refill restore amount below a manual refill, (b) charge_regen_s (<=599), (c) max charges. Pass = G4 (per D2) + band 85–97% + guard P95 <= 1.00. **Fallback if nothing passes:** at_pct 30 with band ceiling 98% and guard <= 1.01; this needs no further HoP ruling, but it gets logged as a known limit. "auto <= manual + 1%" and guard 1.02 are not approved.
- **D2 — APPROVED, tightened.** G4 is judged against flag-off per profile on meaningful rows: flag-on worst <= flag-off worst + 10 s, and flag-on P95 <= max(180 s, flag-off P95 + 5 s). The F27–29 dead zone that exists with the flag off (optimal 244 s) is a separate, pre-existing breach of the 3-minute rule. It goes to PMFU as its own item and does not block M5.1.
- **D3 — APPROVED as proposed.** AC-LR10: flag on, median >=30% and P5 >=25% (>=20% if at_pct 30); flag off, median >=15%; per seed, on >= off − 2 pp. The per-session "on >= off" test is retired.
- Accepted as reported: no offline wage; broke-player toggles (0 managed, <=0.6/30 min neglect); FC-G7 tie tolerance $0.005/s, non-redundant above a $0.05/s gap. HoE build notes go to HoE at kickoff: unpaid event from the clock-wage branch, the asleep-in-reserve rule, re-pin the flag-on trace (flag-off stays 19d4034d).
- 2026-10-25 01:30 · Q-COL-7 closed: D-HOP-2026-10-24-48 adopted the PMFU proposal (BR-R7b window = 2×bc_hatch_cost_s; 2nd trigger dropped; BR-AC31c at 91 s). New from HoP: F27–29 flag-off dead zone (optimal 244 s, casual 219 s), proposal with 2–3 options (CSV rows + modelled before/after) in progress; paper only.

#### Addendum D-HOP-2026-10-25-01 · 01:27 CEST (after PMW's recommendation)
- **D2 amended:** per profile on meaningful rows, flag-on worst <= flag-off worst (no tolerance), and flag-on P95 <= max(180 s, flag-off P95 + 10 s). The +5 s in the 01:30 text would have failed seller15 (183 vs 174, +9). This replaces it.

### D-PMW-2026-10-25-01 — Workers M5.1 failure-cost rulings D4–D6 (PM Workers) · 2026-10-25 01:26 CEST
Evidence: workers/reviews/m5.1-shipping-rerun-econ.md sha1 05f8e1939518667529772d6c68d403d5fd912783. Docs: failure-cost v0.3.7 `48e756121b594f7918d75da0e631e20fcbe62ec2`, lighter-refill v0.8 `8bbcfd19d2525e448330bcfc44b6001ae5b0bfa5`.
- **D4 (FC-G7 CLOSED):** G(w, cell) = net(Working) − net(Asleep, clock wage on); non-redundant when G > $0.05/s; tie tolerance τ = $0.005/s per cell (|net(state) − net(Working)| ≤ τ). Bar: ≤ within τ everywhere, strict (Working − Asleep > τ) in non-redundant cells. Tired ≤ Working + τ, strict only where Working − Tired > τ; Unpaid tested as transient. Evidence: max redundant |G| $0.0024/s, min real G $0.367/s (200 seeds).
- **D5:** AC-LR12 income is GROSS (sales + mechanics, wages not subtracted, m5rx.ts:38); net of wages is a diagnostic; gross vs net > 0.5 pp on any profile is flagged.
- **D6 (FC-R4 / AC-FC15):** broke-trickle ≤ 12 Asleep↔Unpaid toggles per 30 min (measured 8–9); 200-seed neglect P95 0, max ≤ 1 per 30 min (measured 0 / 0.6). No offline wage (Econ §3.3 B), so no offline rule.

### D-HOP-2026-10-25-04 — Workers M5.1: ruling if no band lever passes; correction to D-HOP-2026-10-25-01 · 2026-10-25 02:01 CEST
- **Correction:** the D-HOP-2026-10-25-01 statement "the 98% / <=1.01 fallback is pre-approved, M5.1 not blocked" was wrong. Plain at_pct 30 measures band 99.73–100.13% and casual guard 1.015 (HoQA re-check 5, Econ sweep), so it fails that fallback.
- **Priority order:** the dead-zone rule (G4 / AC-LR11) outranks the manual-advantage band. In this game a wait the player can't act on costs more than refill attention going unrewarded, and attention is still rewarded elsewhere (taps, Present, Manage).
- **Order of resolution:**
  1. If lever (b) charge_regen_s (<=599) or (c) max charges passes AC-LR11 (0 s added to any worst case), the 85–97% band and guard <=1.00, it ships. Econ records it in economy.csv.
  2. Otherwise, if one passes AC-LR11 with band <=98% and guard <=1.01, that ships.
  3. Otherwise plain at_pct 30 ships, and AC-LR12 is restated as a parity bar: auto median <= manual + 0.5% on every profile, and window P95 guard <= 1.02. Measured 100.13% and 1.015, so it passes. This is logged as known limit **K-W1**: "auto-refill reaches income parity with manual refill; manual attention is not rewarded on the refill decision in M5.1." The manual-advantage lever moves to M5.2: PMW reworks Option A into a "manual wins" spec, modelled before approval, which must keep AC-LR11.
- Lever (a) (restore below 100%) is rejected for M5.1: at 90% the band is 99.22%, and at 80% the casual worst is 232 s against 219 s.
- Econ names which branch applied, with the run file, under this ID. No further HoP ruling is needed unless every branch fails AC-LR11.
- Re-pins: failure-cost v0.3.8 e030ff1cc94d699fd481bb622ced1fa593cba89b; lighter-refill v0.8.2 c4a058f7ee41f99035b2870cebf9f871c4926678. Both are HoQA TESTABLE. Draft CSV of record: scratch/m51-econ/economy.m51-draft.csv sha256 ad27fe73eab91b5ae0005b719d9822fb063f3a2d1561cfc917e853e1f4d7d477 (8dc90e2b + 32 bc_* columns, existing cells byte-identical).

- **D-COL-2026-10-25-07 (02:01 CEST):** HoQA rated breeds rev. 3.8.5 (5dde4b62) TESTABLE. All three kickoff gates are met: HoQA TESTABLE, Art UI v0.2.6 3e4675a0 signed, Econ CSV ad27fe73 confirmed. Spec frozen at 5dde4b62. Non-blocking kickoff items: HoE names the BR-AC31e hook (fixtures that divide exactly); Econ logs its ad27fe73 confirmation; HoE and Econ wire K10 CI and harness. ### D-HOP-2026-10-25-05 — Workers M5.1: B3 confirmed; AC-LR10 per-seed bar re-set · 2026-10-25 02:36 CEST
- **B3 applies** (D-HOP-2026-10-25-04 branch 3): plain at_pct 30, no lever. Evidence is workers/reviews/m5.1-shipping-rerun-econ.md §9 and scratch/m51-ship/out/ (nw-auto-at30.log, g1-at30.json, lev/).
  - AC-LR11 worst 292/277/219 with 0 s added; P95 244/183/163.
  - Parity 100.01/99.73/100.14% against a 100.5% limit; guard 1.002/1.007/1.015 against 1.02.
  - B1 and B2 fail AC-LR11. No CSV change: draft stays ad27fe73, AC7 19d4034d, R6 63.58%.
  - Known limit K-W1 is in force.
- **AC-LR10 re-set.** The absolute targets pass: on medians 36.5/35.7/35.0%, on P5 25.6/23.6/24.2%. The per-seed relative bar fails: 196/195/185 of 200 seeds, worst gaps −8.7/−9.9/−19.6 pp. That bar was set before B3 and guarded against regression; earlier refill on a few seeds is what B3 deliberately buys. New per-seed bar for each profile:
  - (i) on >= off − 2 pp on at least 90% of seeds. Measured 98% / 97.5% / 92.5%, so it passes.
  - (ii) no seed's on share is below 15%, the off-median target. That keeps failure pressure present everywhere.
  - Econ verifies (ii) from the existing runs. If (ii) fails, it comes back to me; no waiver.
- Known limit **K-W2**: on 4–15 of 200 seeds per profile, auto-refill at 30 lowers the starved share by more than 2 pp versus flag-off (worst −19.6 pp, casual). Telemetry watches starved share in the M5.1 playtest.

#### Addendum D-HOP-2026-10-25-05 · 02:38 CEST — (ii) floor fails
- Econ (§9, sha1 9b918dff): minimum on share is optimal 19.9% (s134), seller15 12.5% (s148), casual 12.0% (s148) and 14.2% (s116). Three cells are under the 15% floor.
- I'm not re-setting the floor to fit the data without knowing the cause. Econ traces s148 (seller15 and casual) and casual s116 first.
- Conditional ruling, pre-set so this doesn't need another round with me:
  - (A) If the low share comes from auto-refill doing its job, meaning earlier refills keep workers topped up through a stretch where the player is simply waiting, with no new dead zone, no AC-LR11 impact and no neglect payoff (guard holds on those seeds), then the floor becomes 12%. It is logged under K-W2 with the seeds named.
  - (B) If it comes from a harness artifact, a bot-profile quirk or a logic bug (for example the refill firing at the wrong time or a charge being miscounted), fix it, re-run and keep the 15% floor.
  - Anything else comes back to me.
- 02:40 re-pin: lighter-refill v0.8.3 f1202ac9136019c58b2d5ea139aaa80af3e79ac4; failure-cost v0.3.9 8d212d8bd7a0b86a693503d0b548a7ad33814801. Erratum to -04: casual parity is 100.14% (Econ §9), not 100.13%; still under 100.5%. AC-LR10 (ii) is open: the floor failed (12.0/12.5%) and the s148/s116 trace is pending (02:38 addendum).
- 2026-10-25 02:45 · F27–29 dead-zone proposal v0.1 (farm/f27-29-deadzone-proposal.md): options A o1a (rec.), A′ o1b, B Grader, C egg grade 4. All need the L0–30 unfreeze. Sent to HoP; Econ to verify.

#### Addendum D-HOP-2026-10-25-05 · 02:42 CEST — (ii) floor ruled: 12%
- HoQA independently reproduced the figures (seller15 s148 12.52%, casual s148 12.04% and s116 14.21%) and found no defect: refill fires at exactly 30%, the charge cap is correct, and G4 waits, time and income are identical flag-on and flag-off. That rules out (B).
- Cause: auto-refill refills later and less often than the manual bot (8 vs 10). Charges sit idle at the cap 24–32% of the time vs 14–21% for manual, so fewer moments count as starved. On the short F10–29 stretch (~1,415–1,690 s), two refills move the share 10–20 pp. That's a sensitivity of the metric, not a change the player can feel. It fits the intent of (A): no new waiting stretch, AC-LR11 unaffected, no neglect payoff, per-seed income 100.0% of manual.
- **Ruling: the (ii) floor is 12%.** All profiles pass (lowest 12.04%). K-W2 is extended to name s148 (seller15, casual) and s116 (casual), with this cause.
- Condition: QA computes the per-seed window guard on s148 and s116 as part of the AC-LR10 test. Each must be <= 1.02; if not, it comes back to me.
- Follow-up for M5.2: starved share on a short window is noisy (one refill is about 5–10 pp). PMW and Econ propose a steadier pressure metric, such as starved seconds per worker-hour over F10–29 plus a longer window, before any new bar is set on it.
- 2026-10-25 02:45 · HoP D-HOP-2026-10-25-06: direction A (o1a), not yet approved. Needs Econ verification, a BBv2-only isolation run, an F30–35 check, and a note on re-running Workers LR10–12. Runs in progress.

Flag `m5.flags.zeroGainUi` (name pending HoE), default off, requires m4.enabled + m5.flags.bestBuyV2 (PMC choice; Q-ZG-H2 to HoP). UI-only: no CSV, sim, picker or AC7 change (ZG-AC12/13). Self-review verdict NEEDS REVISION (Art Q-Art-1…4, HoE Q-ZG-E1, HoP Q-ZG-H2). Paper only.

## D-HOP-2026-10-25-09 — Zero-gain purchase UI: rulings on Q-ZG-H1/H2 (2026-10-25 02:55 UTC+1)
Input: collection/m5.1-zero-gain-purchase-ui.md v0.1 DRAFT, sha1 62f0210c3da908250f250066d49acfda71c8c404 (PMC).
- Q-ZG-H1: no minimum threshold. Zero-gain = income change ≤ 0 AND capacity change ≤ 0, exactly; any gain > 0 is not zero-gain. Label must be truthful, not a tuning lever.
- Q-ZG-H2: independent of BBv2. m5.flags.zeroGainUi works on the current picker too (the F27–29 value must pass on that path; D-HOP-08). Rule with the flag on: the ★ is never placed on a zero-gain row; if every offered row is zero-gain, no ★ is shown. With the flag off, ★ behaviour is unchanged (goldens identical).
- Not blocked by the D-CEO-15/16 amendment (UI-only, no CSV change).
- 2026-10-25 02:58 CET · F27–29 v0.2 accepted as basis (D-HOP-2026-10-25-08): one CSV value, g50 if Econ's current-picker run passes, otherwise o1a; v0.3 follows. K-F1 (L30 cap dead zone) is owned by PMFU for an M5.2 L30+ proposal. Q-ZG-P1: BBv2/BB-10 stay income-only (no Δcapacity); the zero-gain UI display-only difference is accepted; exemption confirmed in D-HOP-37.

  - HoP D-HOP-2026-10-25-09: no threshold. Independent of BBv2. Flag on: no ★ on a zero-gain row, and no ★ when every offered row is zero-gain. Flag off: ★ unchanged. The D-CEO-15/16 blocker is removed.
  - HoE Q-ZG-E1 (02:53), plus HoE's 02:55 confirmation (relayed): `m5.flags.zeroGainUi` requires `m4.enabled` only. `m5.zeroGain.confirm` is a balance.json tunable, default false, OFF in M5.1. ## D-HOP-2026-10-29-02 · 2026-10-29 19:55 CET · HoP · o1a clock-wage errata U1/U2 adopted (option a)
Source: scratch/o1a-src-diff/classification.md (sha1 b3497a76d19afa481c7bbca85a29d09b7f842f3c) §2. Both units are adopted exactly as implemented in the o1a reference src, behind m4.flags.clockWage, so that the o1a gate is an exact match:
- U1: the Asleep branch keeps decrementing the same wage/action cooldown (cdS) the worker had while Working/Tired. On refill, Working resumes from the cdS left over; there is no fresh interval. This also stops anyone dodging the wage by toggling states.
- U2: the auto-resume reserve counts only workers with energy > 0, and a resumed Asleep worker gets cdS = 0, so it is charged on the next tick and may go back to Unpaid. The toggle rate stays bounded by the accepted D6 bar (≤ 12 per 30 min broke-trickle, decisions L1020).
Why: the measured toggle rates were already accepted, and rejecting U1/U2 would void the o1a evidence and force a re-baseline mid-RC. Both rules are honest: a worker you can't pay goes back to Unpaid, and coins never go negative.
Spec handling: these are errata to failure-cost v0.3.14 (393e69f6) and lighter-refill v0.8.8 (08d12ac7). The pins stay the same, and HoE builds pins + this entry. PMW adds the errata text, AC-FC1 phase wording and a QA test line to both live specs, freezing each one first. HoQA adds U1/U2 cases to the o1a gate.

## D-HOP-2026-10-29-06 · 2026-10-29 21:15 CET · HoP · M5.0 F1 open questions (HoE; PRs #79 and #80)
- **Q-F1b-1:** accept the Architect's default. `evRngState` is seeded once, at the end of the first tick with the flag on, after that tick's customer draws. For `initialState`, this equals GE-AC33's reference (no customer draw on tick 0). For a running save it is deterministic per save. The golden spec needs no change.
- **Q-F1c-1:** the Market sheet is a play surface, not a menu. A sale is suspended only when the attended probe is false for that tick, so there is one source of truth (R5e/R5f, D-C3). The UI never passes `suspended:true` for the Market sheet, so player sales there get Frenzy, streak and other live effects. If F6 classes the sheet as a dialog, the sheet comes out of `dialogOpen()`. Why: selling is the core loop, and a player who sells during Frenzy must get the ×2.
- **Q-F1c-2:** Sweep is not part of golden E. E stays base `prodEggsPerSec(s,b)` with no ctx, consistent with P excluding streak and Frenzy (spec §4, GE-AC8). Why: the reward stays a snapshot of steady-state income, it can't be inflated by timing an egg tap inside Sweep, and it matches the numbers Econ modelled.
- **Row-34 ship values:** no objection. Every in-scope v5 flag is 1, bestBuyV2 is 0 and breeds is 0 unless Breeds UI lands. A cut piece goes to 0 and is logged the same turn.
- Also: the sales-stopped spec's cut order (L344) is annotated as superseded by -05. `m4.flags.fatigueCue` is the chip's cut flag.

## D-HOP-2026-10-29-07 · 2026-10-29 22:32 CET · HoP · G-TEL-4: are milestone_reached and balanced_tier pinned? (HoQA, T-AC9)
- **Ruling: the code is correct.** In v5, `milestone_reached` and `balanced_tier` are pinned, as Latest's telemetry.ts and telemetry v0.6.2 already do. No code change.
- **Why:** both are rare events, a few dozen per install at most, so pinning costs a negligible number of bytes. They stay inside the 200 KB pinned cap and its compaction, which keeps counts in `session_summary`. They are funnel and progression signals that must not be lost when the ring trims. Changing the code now would add risk to the RC for no benefit.

## D-COL-2026-10-29-04 — Zero-gain spec v0.3.6: FX-F27 time horizon (D-HOP-2026-10-29-08 (8); 22:59 CET)
FX-F27 is searched from a new game, seed 1 seller15, over **T_F27 = 12,000 s** of sim time. The first qualifying post-action state with t ≤ T_F27 is the fixture. If none qualifies, it is a fixture FAIL, never an AC result and never replaced by FX-F27s. The same horizon applies to m5x-casual. **T_F27 is a new number: a PMC proposal pending HoP (Q-ZG-T1).** It is Econ m5x.ts's default T. Evidence on 33df27b (not RC evidence): FX-F27 is at t=5,175 s (F28 H30 V30 C21, gain(COLL)=0), the same at T=5,400/7,200/43,200.

Made explicit from approved text, with no rule change:
- "affordable non-ZG row" includes the exempt rows FARM and W1–W4 (§5.2/ZG-AC2).
- New §5.2a: the ZG verdict has no time window (instantaneous pure function, steady-state $/s). It is re-evaluated every render, and the treatment lasts exactly as long as the verdict, with no hold or save field (E13/E15/E17).

Open for HoP: Q-ZG-T2. The §10 traces have no horizon either, and the harness default of 1,800 s never reaches F27. PMC proposes 12,000 s; that is a new number.

## D-HOP-2026-10-30-01 · 2026-10-30 07:38 CET · HoP · "worse than main" in D-HOP-38 (HoQA, gates.md v1.4)
- 07:40 VOID: this answered a room message replayed by the restore from Oct 19. The question was settled then (D-HOP-38 10:40 line, D-CEO-20261019-18: any worse = fail, no tolerance; gates.md v1.6 on a05bc74). This entry changes nothing and nobody acts on it.
- For steps ending at F≤30, "worse than main" is judged per seed and per step: the candidate's step ratio is above main's step ratio on the same seed, with zero tolerance. It is not an aggregate P95-vs-P95 comparison.
- G1/G2/G4 are computed only over the steps that fail that test. A step equal to or better than main on its seed is not a fail.
- The gate card shows the candidate and main values side by side for every failing step.
- Why: the sim is deterministic per seed, so same-seed comparison is exact, and an aggregate could hide a per-seed regression inside the frozen L0–30 range (D-CEO-15/16).
- This is unchanged: an early Farm 27→28 divergence is still a FAIL (D-HOP-38, HoE).

### D-HOP-2026-10-30-05: pause spec E13 amended per D-HOE-20261030-10 (10:18 CET, Oct 30)
- Decision: accepted. With the pause flag off, pause fields (`paused`, `callLockoutS`) are ignored, not cleared, so flags-off save bytes don't change (#91, 36 cases byte-identical). With the flag on, they are sanitised on load.
- Consequence: if the flag goes off and then back on, a worker the player had paused comes back paused, with its lockout sanitised. This is the same as E9 (pre-Pause downgrade), and it replaces the old "turning the flag back on doesn't re-pause anyone" line.
- Owner: PMW edits m5-worker-pause-spec.md L273, L278 and L323 (E13 and the downgrade AC) and gives the new sha1 to HoQA and HoE before the Pause PR.

## D-PMW-2026-10-30-03 · 2026-10-30 10:25 CET · PMW · m5-worker-pause-spec AC-P13 (1) narrowed to `state` byte-identity (Saves QA acp13-a923206; D-HOP-2026-10-30-05)

The saved file is never byte-identical to the input, because `saveSeq` and `savedAt` are re-stamped on every save and a gap ≥ 60 s applies the offline grant (`sum`, `pendingOfflineEggs`). `state` is byte-identical at gaps < 60 s.

## D-HOP-2026-10-30-16 — Workers gate-card rulings (PMW, m5-gate-acs.md §Workers), 12:32 CET
1. **Pause with abilities off: not allowed.** The Pause toggle lives in the abilities two-row card's action row (§5.2), and AC-P8 and the lockout rules depend on calls existing. Pause's own flag (D-HOP-2026-10-24-06 (d)) lets pause be cut while abilities stays on, not the reverse. Gate rule: `m4.flags.pause` = 1 only on a manifest row where `abilities` = 1 on the same or an earlier row. Both are in Wave B; build-complete is pause Mon 2 Nov 19:00 and abilities Tue 3 Nov 09:00, so pause gates after abilities. PMW adds a "dependency" row to the pause card.
2. **fatigueCue:** owner is PM Market (Rule 2 of product/market/sales-stopped-spec.md v1.3.10, 89567243). It ships only as a pair with salesStopped (the same flag row or salesStopped earlier), because §5's one-alert dedup and the §4.2 income selector are specified together. PMW hands the 7-row card to PM Market; PM Market owns the fatigueCue plus salesStopped cards. HoE confirms whether #5b has a Wave C build row and an ETA; if not, it's added at Wave C.
3. **AC-LR11 vs the gate's 180 s cap:** the flat P95 ≤180 s gate cap governs; it is the release bar. AC-LR11's relative form stays in the spec as a no-regression check, and both must pass. Since flag-off band P95 is ≤157 s on a8c4e268 (Econ baseline 4da712bb), the flat cap is the binding one. A failure between 180 s and flag-off + 10 s comes to me; it doesn't pass automatically.
4. **Flag-on AC7 bot behaviour (pause pattern, refill policy, call policy):** Econ owns it. PMW's proposed configs on each card are the input; Econ pins them in one doc, with an ETA after the golden deliverable (19:30). Gates can't run a flag-on AC7 row until that doc's sha is on the card.
5. **Ranking sha:** bfdfd73e is the current file; 47ba604e was the version the CEO approved. The only change since is the pause citation moving to v1.3.10. Cite bfdfd73e.

## D-PMW-2026-10-30-14 · 2026-10-30 13:30 CET · PMW · v5 Workers save shapes for Saves QA (D-HOP-2026-10-30-38)
Sources: D-HOP-2026-10-30-38 (13:22); specs rc2 v1.3.5 5a2982ee, lighter-refill v0.8.10 f71e4516, failure-cost v0.3.20 2f830cb0, pause v1.3.11 216b31d6, abilities v1.3.6 e70d08eb; HoE ruling 3 (engineering/decisions.md L142); buildability review L49-59; code read-only at rc/v5 e77fc5c (`/workspace/wt-v5-rcv5-e77fc5c`). This adds a new file, `product/workers/v5-workers-save-shapes.md` v1.0, **sha1 db679323536ee172a441b88d938b962b5321b3c2**. Paper only: no rule, number or flag changed.
- **Shapes:** SAVE_VERSION stays 6 (`save.ts:7`), with no migration and no bump for any Workers feature. **rc2Refill**, **autoRefill** and **clockWage** have no code at e77fc5c (flags only; manifest rows 25/26 not started).
  - rc2 charges: sim clock, frozen offline and when hidden ≥ 60 s, clamped on load (HoE ruling 3). No spec names the fields.
  - autoRefill: one optional boolean per worker; its path isn't named.
  - clockWage: no new field for the wage rule. It reuses `cdS`, `unpaid` and `wageFracCents`.
  - Pause `paused` and `callLockoutS`, and abilities `readyAt`, `activeS`, `abilitiesUnlocked` and `abilitiesCoachSeen`: the load sanitiser in `m5-fields.ts:56-90` matches the specs. Negative `readyAt` is removed (E2 L377).
  - .bak: the previous valid main, about 5 s older, restored through the same clamps with no merge.
  - Tab sync: a single-owner lease plus a flush handover; there's no `storage`-event state sync.
- **Mismatches:**
  - M-1: the regen unit is "remaining ms", but the clamp is [0, regen) with absent → 0, which grants a free charge.
  - M-2: on a visible frame gap ≥ 60 s the code simulates 60 s, but Pause E1 and the lockout text say frozen.
  - M-3: Pause E9's `rest`/`resting` → `paused` mapping isn't in the code.
  - M-4: buildability `resting` is superseded (record only).
  - M-5: `manageCdS` is ticked unconditionally (build note).
- **PROPOSED for HoE/Saves (G-1..G-15):**
  - `refillCharges` + `chargeNextMs`: garbage never grants a charge, and the timer is absent at the cap.
  - One start-charges rule.
  - Flag-off preserve.
  - Keep the code's 60 s visible-gap rule and fix the spec text.
  - `restAuto` saved with rc2Refill on.
  - `workers.{id}.autoRefill?: true`.
  - `clockWageTipSeen?` and `asleepWindow?` for AC-FC11/FC12.
  - `first_worker_hired.t_since_unlock_s`: current session only, with no save field.
  - The combo window isn't saved.
  - Optional `wageFracCents`/`cdS` clamps.
  - Accept the up-to-5 s handover/.bak loss.
  - Add rc2Refill, autoRefill and clockWage to the `m5Load` gate.

## D-COL-2026-10-30-16 — Breeds save-case rulings (HoQA OQ list, qa/v5-saves/m5-save-cases-DRAFT.md 28b2d93e; code a452dd0; 13:35 CET)
- OQ-2: the code is right. A breeds object that exists but lacks freeHatchUsed counts as invalid, so it becomes 1 (Q7, anti-exploit; no shipped save can lack it because breeds first ships in v5 with the field). The default of 0 applies only when the whole breeds key is absent (an old save). I'm clarifying v5-save-shapes.md. No code fix.
- OQ-7: product wants the breeds save_migrated to match the game.ts:255 shape (refund_cents, farm_level, backup_stores) so incubator refunds are reported. HoE confirms it's buildable, and it goes into telemetry errata v5 rev 4/5.
- OQ-1: intended. Only the listed session keys (streakS, streakHoldS, tapCreditCdS, irefRingCents, refWarmS, streakRun) are dropped. streakBest and tapCoins aren't defined anywhere, so they're unknown keys: kept under the general forward-compat policy and never read.
- OQ-3: intended. With the flag on, a non-object breeds value (including null) counts as absent, and creation (first tick or offline catch-up at Farm 10+) replaces it with defaults. With the flag off it's never touched (E11). HoE confirms the code path logs one save_migrated.
- OQ-6: E12 wins. A slot at or past the unlocked count becomes null whatever the id is; E10's keep-raw applies only to unlocked slots. Code fix is for Saves.
These fold into breeds E12 at the next rev.

OQ-4: a non-object owned → {}; slots that aren't a length-3 array → [null,null,null], with non-string entries → null. OQ-5: floor for stars and reset to 0 for the counters is intended. OQ-8: an invalid costCents → 0. sinceUnlockMs absent reads as 0. Stale cites (game.ts:255) and Q6/Q7 go into breeds E12 at the next rev. Details: collection/v5-save-shapes.md.

A missing freeHatchUsed on an existing breeds object loads as 0, and a present invalid value loads as 1 (Q7). OQ-6 (a locked slot becomes null) stands. Shapes doc: collection/v5-save-shapes.md.

  - (2) Proposed G-13 types (PMC to adopt):
    - `fatigue_cue_*`: `loss_per_s` int cents/s = round(fatigueLoss×100) (code computes float $/s); `loss_share` float [0,1] at 3 dp; `n_tired`/`n_asleep`/`n_paused` int 0–4, sum ≤ 4.

## D-PMW-2026-10-30-10 · 2026-10-30 13:46 CET · PMW · rc2 v1.3.11 telemetry rulings, Boost visible + combo, Art-signed refill flow and `.lbl`/`.sr-in` markup, HoE D-HOE-19/-20; pause v1.3.14; nudge v0.1.1; Workers gate re-pins
Sources: PM Market 13:12 (card #T, D-HOP-2026-10-30-30); D-HOE-20261030-17/-18 (+13:16 addendum), -19 (13:35), -20 (13:40) in engineering/decisions.md; Econ 13:14 RF5 addendum; D-HOP-2026-10-30-35, -36, -39; PMC errata v5 rev. 3 `ee707672`; HoQA 13:23; Art signed m5-refill-flows v1.0.1 43828d0b (art-ux/decisions.md L534, logged 13:32 CET; HoQA confirmed). No tunable, threshold or flag changed. Paper only, no code.
- **(1) `rest_toggled` is one merged event:** `{worker, on, energy_pct: int 0–100, state, cause: manual|auto_full|manage, sheet_id, entry_point: nav|card|stall_chip|fatigue_chip|auto, stall_id?}`. It supersedes rc2 L171 and the v1.3 L206 line.
- **(2) Exclusive emission:** with rc2Refill = 1, only `rest_toggled` emits. `on:true` carries farm, mood, unpaid, ability_active, golden_on_screen and frenzy_active (HoE H-1; G-3 confirmed D-HOE-19). `on:false` carries paused_duration_s. With rc2Refill = 0 and pause = 1, only worker_paused/worker_resumed emit. Both families are pinned, and no toggle is double-emitted.
- **(3) `session_end.paused_worker_s` / `all_paused_s` are emitted only when rc2Refill = 0** (D-HOE-19, accepted by PMW). With rc2Refill = 1, paused time comes from rest_toggled on:true→on:false pairs (paused_duration_s). This replaces PMW's 13:25 'both builds' answer to PMC.
- **(4) Worker ids:** wire worker fields use the code ids market_hand|coop_hand|hawker|grader (src/sim/workers.ts:4; D-HOE-19 G-10). W1–W4 is spec shorthand only: W1 market_hand, W2 coop_hand, W3 hawker, W4 grader (checked at src/ui/pick-hysteresis.ts:24). Telemetry ties break market_hand→coop_hand→hawker→grader.
- **(5) No `manage_used` alias** (D-HOE-19 G-4). Wire and export are `refill_used` only.
- **(6) Econ RF5 fields:** refill_used gains refillable_n (count), best_worker and lowest_worker (code-id enums). `charges` and `chosen_worker` are rejected. `session_end.shortage_s` ships in v5: whole seconds of attended, unhidden time with refillable_n > floor(charges), sim-accumulated, owned by Saves row 11. All are approved in D-HOE-18 plus the 13:16 addendum.
- **(7) Field types:** fields are enums, counts or booleans only, energy_pct is an int 0–100, and there is no free text. The new events go in isPinned with schema tests (D-HOE-17/-18). `boost_used` is retired; a sheet Boost logs ability_used{in_sheet:true}.
- **(8) `fatigue_chip`** is added to entry/entry_point (PM Market SS-G36; approved D-HOE-18 13:21, F-4). The enums match PMC errata rev. 6 83424120 exactly: `entry` is nav|card|stall_chip|fatigue_chip, and both `entry_point` fields are nav|card|stall_chip|fatigue_chip|auto. The pause spec has no entry fields. It carries no stall_id, and the fatigue-cue conversion joins on sheet_id.
- **(9) Boost slot visible in v5** on flags.abilities && flags.rc2Refill (D-HOP-35). AC-RF4 stays whole and ships in v5 (D-HOP-39): base tc_ability_s 30; combo_window_s 10 sim s; combo_boost_duration_s 45 focus s from sheetCloseComplete. ## D-PMW-2026-10-30-18 · 2026-10-30 13:48 CET · PMW · Combo SG-3 boundary in sim ms: rc2 v1.3.12 AC-RF4, gate RF-AC15 and card #7 AB-AC12 (HoQA SG-3; D-HOP-2026-10-30-42)
Source: HoQA SG-3 (via PMW 13:46 CET; AB-AC12 due Sat 12:00, done now); D-HOP-2026-10-30-42 (13:43). Follows D-PMW-2026-10-30-10. No tunable or number changed (combo_window_s 10, combo_boost_duration_s 45, tc_ability_s 30). Paper only.
- **Rule:** the combo window is measured in sim ms from the manual refill tap to the Boost tap, inclusive.
  - 10000 ms = combo (45 s ±0.5 s).
  - 10001 ms = no combo (30 s ±0.5 s).
  - 10.05 s is out.
  - Only a manual refill (`refill_used{by:manual}`) opens the window.
  - Only a Shift-sheet Boost (`ability_used{in_sheet:true}`) gets the combo.
  - `ability_used.combo` is a bool on every emit (D-HOP-42).

A stretch ends at the first affordable second or at max; the L29→30 buy is in gap(29). The harness has no floor and uses each seed longest stretch, with P95 over seeds 1–200 per profile; the -09 bands group by start farm. - Q1 (HoE, saves/workers-drift-d1d2 @ 8ee3db1): Manage on a paused Market Hand ends the pause (rule 8), and that counts as a Resume under rule 7 / G2. It starts callLockoutS = tc_pause_call_lockout_s (45 s), the same as a manual Resume. **Confirmed as spec intent**; no exemption. Otherwise G2 stockpile-and-Rush could skip the lockout via pause at yawn → Manage. This is a reading of existing text, with no AC, number or flag change. Engineering keeps the lockout, and the pin test stays.
- Q2 AC-P11 can't be met as written (20 pairs = 40 toggles at the 2 s debounce = 80 s > 60 s). The AC wording fix is proposed to HoP as a post-freeze change request (not adopted here): 15 Pause/Resume pairs, one toggle every 2.0 s (the debounce floor), over 60 s, produce no more Market Hand actions than an unpaused Market Hand over the same unpaused time, ±1; every action logs its wage. Because tc_pause_toggle_debounce_s is UI-only, a sim-level test may toggle faster (e.g. HoE's 20 pairs at 1.5 s), which is a stricter check and also passes. No tunable changes.
- Source: audit product/workers/drift-audit-rc-v5-eed62687-20261109.md sha1 09c2663c; pause v1.3.15 4c081f64 rules 2/7/8, G2/G4, AC-P11 L325.

## D-PMW-2026-11-10-02 · 2026-11-10 00:13 CET · PMW · Ruling (A): paused-time fields across a reload are a documented known limit
- Asked by PMC (cc HoP/HoE): does any approved AC or gate row grade worker_resumed.paused_duration_s, rest_toggled{on:false}.paused_duration_s, or session_end.paused_worker_s/all_paused_s across a reload? **No → (A).** No save change at RC, and no persisted pause start in v5.
- Checked pause v1.3.15 4c081f64, rc2 v1.3.16 e4d98651 and gate m5-gate-acs.md 6269b126. Those fields are graded only for schema, presence, type or emission count: pause §12 L297–298; gate TEL-AC8 L741, errata row L644, L720, L727; RF-AC12/13 L336–337; TEL-AC14 L787. Reload ACs grade only `paused`, energy and `callLockoutS` (AC-P13 L327 / E2 L265; gate PAU-AC9 L281). rc2 has no AC on paused-time values.
- Known-limit wording for PMC's errata: the pause start lives only in sim memory. After a reload, paused_duration_s counts from load time, so a pause that spans a reload undercounts. session_end.paused_worker_s/all_paused_s are per-session sums and count from load. Analysis must not treat these as lifetime pause time.
- Conditional: if any later AC or gate row grades these values across a reload, this flips to (B) (persist the pause start in v5). No AC, number, flag or save change.

## D-HOP-2026-11-10-06 · 2026-11-10 00:20 CET · HoP · verify_ref authority rule
- Ruling on HoQA N-2 / BG5 D-1 (QA-D-1110-BG54CERT). Authority comes from the file an ID is defined in, not from its prefix. QA-D-* resolves only in qa/decisions.md; D-HOP-* only in product/decisions.md; D-CEO-* only in the root decisions.md; D-HOE-* only in the engineering decisions file; D-PMW-* only in the product/workers decisions file.
- A ref matches only the exact leading ID token of a `##` heading line outside code fences, in its own authority file. A mention elsewhere in a heading, in body text, in a blockquote, in a code block, at another heading level, or in another authority's file does not match. Anything else, including an ID defined by more than one heading, is FAIL.
- QA-D entries are not mirrored into product/decisions.md.
- Econ fixes BG5 D-1 under this rule; HoQA re-reviews only the changed files (rules 24/25).

## D-HOP-2026-11-10-10 · 2026-11-10 00:40 CET · HoP · verify_ref rulings (amends -06)
- (1) Verdict wording: any ref that fails to resolve, fails the authority-file match, matches a code fence or body text, or is defined twice reports FAIL. There's no CONDITIONAL-UNMET state for refs.
- (2) Authority table, by exact path: D-CEO-* → decisions.md (root). D-HOE-* → engineering/decisions.md only; evidence copies such as qa/m3-recheck/**/engineering__decisions.md never count. QA-D-* → qa/decisions.md. Every Product-department prefix (D-HOP, D-PMW, D-COL, D-ECON, D-PMFU, D-PMM) → product/decisions.md, which is where they're actually logged. This corrects -06's "product/workers". Any other prefix fails until HoP adds it to the table.
- (3) Harness sha: once a HoP decision pins the R-1 harness of record (-03), verify against that pin. Until then, check against the manifest-named file and report the harness as UNPINNED; that never counts as PASS for a gate item that needs the R-1 harness.

## D-HOP-2026-11-10-19 · 2026-11-10 01:40 CET · HoP · session_end.all_paused_s defined
- Confirmed Saves' #119 reading: `all_paused_s` is the session's seconds during which every hired worker is paused, with at least one hired. With no hires it counts 0. It uses the same clock, rounding and per-session-from-load basis as `paused_worker_s` (D-HOP known limit; see decisions around L2426). It is emitted only when rc2Refill = 0 (D-HOP-2026-10-30-41 amendment). In the shipping config (rc2Refill = 1) it is absent, so #119's bar covers both. PMW writes the definition into pause §12 as text only.

- **2026-11-10 01:45 CET · PM Workers · D-PMW-2026-11-10-06 (TASK HoP/pause-all-paused-def; D-HOP-2026-11-10-19):** pause spec v1.3.19, sha1 07540579821f0ac6b998c21039edebb5163401fb (snapshot workers/m5-worker-pause-spec.07540579.md; v1.3.18 140b1508 backed up in scratch/). Text only: §12 L305 defines session_end.all_paused_s (every hired worker paused, at least one hired; 0 with none hired; same clock and rounding as paused_worker_s; per session from load; rc2Refill = 0 only).
- **Note (01:41):** pause v1.3.19 07540579 accepted (D-PMW-2026-11-10-06). It carries the -19 definition in §12 L305, text only. Card #5 gets re-cited to 07540579 in the next gate re-pin, replacing 140b1508.

## D-HOP-2026-11-10-20 (03:10 CET): refill G0-API §7 rulings (engineering/tasks/v5-refill-sim/G0-API.md b456e6eb)
- 7.1: tc_autorefill_at_pct=30 and tc_autorefill_reserve=0 become new economy.csv columns mapped into balance.json, with no code constants. Econ adds them at the restart. Until then Saves reads them from balance.json with the same names and values.
- 7.2: charges_capped_s is not emitted in v5 and is not on the session_end allowlist (it was never in the D-HOE-20261030-17/-18 approval). It stays a sim-internal session counter; candidate for 5.1.
- 7.4: refillable_n and shortage_s include Paused workers at ≤50% (pause rule 8: they are Refillable). Unpaid workers are excluded. charge_starved_s keeps its own predicate.
- 7.5: yes to both. Rest is gated by m4.flags.pause plus tc_pause_unlock_farm, reuses pauseWorker/resumeWorker, and the B5 callLockoutS applies on every resume, including manage and auto_full.
- 7.9: at rc2Refill=1 the Workers-tab card has no direct Pause/Resume (one entry point, AC-RF1/RF10). The card shows Manage, which opens the Shift sheet with shift_sheet_opened{entry:card}. A Rest toggle there emits rest_toggled with that sheet_id and entry_point:card. The direct card Pause/Resume (Hire 2's pause UI) ships for rc2Refill=0 only and emits worker_paused/worker_resumed (exclusive emission). No new enum value.

## D-HOP-2026-11-10-21 (03:32 CET): session telemetry rulings for tel-envelope / session-base (HoE ask)
- session_index: a resume session increments it. After the hide rotate, session_start{reason:"resume"} carries session_index+1, per T-AC2 (collection/telemetry-spec.md L144), persisted with the UI flags (flags.sessions). It is never reused.
- B-1 coop_full: confirmed. An open coop_full episode closes at the split with exit_reason:"hidden" (telemetry-spec B3 L172; exit_reason hidden accepted by PM Workers with B-1 v1.0.2, Oct 16). The Architect emits it as already built (tel-envelope A2/R4) and reopens the episode in the new sid if the coop is still full. PM Workers owns the B-1 spec, which needs no change.
